{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}\omega^{k}+n^{i}\right)\not{\partial}_{\omega^{i}}f_{\sigma}=0,

(63)

where
{\bm{a}},{\bm{n}}
are arbitrary, up to the constraint (
62
).

Remark
.

To express (
63
) in terms of the angles
(\theta,\varphi)
in (
41
), one has to use the transformation

\not{\partial}_{{\bm{\omega}}}=\mathcal{U}\,\left(\begin{array}[]{c}\partial_{\theta}\\
(\sin\theta)^{-1}\partial_{\varphi}\end{array}\right),\quad\mathcal{U}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\cos\theta\cos\varphi&-\sin\varphi\\
\cos\theta\sin\varphi&\cos\varphi\\
-\sin\theta&0\\
\end{array}\right),

(64)

relating the gradient on
S^{2}
in Cartesian and spherical coordinates.

The next step is to choose the vectors
{\bm{a}},{\bm{n}}
in (
63
) in such a way that the constraint (
51
) is satisfied. This step requires computing
\nabla_{b}(T_{f})^{ab}
from (
63
), which we do by using the following lemma.

Lemma 2
.

Let
\psi=\psi(x,u,s)
be given and
\psi_{*}=\psi_{*}(x,{\bm{u}},{\bm{\omega}})
be defined as in (
56
). Assume that
f_{\sigma}
solves (
63
) and that the vector fields
{\bm{a}}f_{\sigma}
,
{\bm{n}}f_{\sigma}
are smooth. Let

\Psi^{\nu}(x)=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times S^{2}}\psi_{*}(x,{\bm{u}},{\bm{\omega}})\,u^{\nu}\,f_{\sigma}\,\frac{d{\bm{u}}\,d{\bm{\omega}}}{u^{0}}.

Then

\displaystyle e^{\beta}_{(\nu)}\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Psi^{\nu}=

\displaystyle\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times S^{2}}[e^{\beta}_{(\nu)}u^{\nu}\partial_{x^{\beta}}\psi_{*}+(F\psi)_{*}+\mathchoice{\gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{\gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{\gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}{\beta}}}}{\gamma^{{{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}_{{\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\alpha}{\beta}}}}u^{\alpha}\psi_{*}]\,f_{\sigma}\,\frac{d{\bm{u}}\,d{\bm{\omega}}}{u^{0}}

\displaystyle+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times S^{2}}\left(\not{\partial}_{{\bm{\omega}}}\cdot{\bm{n}}+\partial_{\bm{u}}\cdot{\bm{a}}-\frac{{\bm{a}}\cdot{\bm{u}}}{(u^{0})^{2}}\right)\psi_{*}f_{\sigma}\,\frac{d{\bm{u}}\,d{\bm{\omega}}}{u^{0}},

where
F
is given by (
60
).

Proof.

See Appendix.
∎

Applying Lemma
2
with
\psi=u^{\mu}
, and using
F(u^{\mu})=\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{\mu}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.86232pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.95248pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.10892pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}u^{\beta}+a^{\mu}
, we find

\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu}=m\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times S^{2}}\left[\left(\not{\partial}_{{\bm{\omega}}}\cdot{\bm{n}}+\partial_{\bm{u}}\cdot{\bm{a}}-\frac{{\bm{a}}\cdot{\bm{u}}}{(u^{0})^{2}}\right)u^{\mu}+a^{\mu}\right]\,f_{\sigma}\,\frac{d{\bm{u}}\,d{\bm{\omega}}}{u^{0}}.

In order to turn the previous equation into an identity valid for all kinetic densities
f_{\sigma}
, we let

\left(\not{\partial}_{{\bm{\omega}}}\cdot{\bm{n}}+\partial_{\bm{u}}\cdot{\bm{a}}-\frac{{\bm{a}}\cdot{\bm{u}}}{(u^{0})^{2}}\right)u^{\mu}+a^{\mu}=\frac{1}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu},

(65)

where
\mathcal{M}_{f}
is the Lorentz invariant mass function

\mathcal{M}_{f}=\mathcal{M}_{f}(x)=m\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times S^{2}}f_{\sigma}\,\frac{d{\bm{u}}\,d{\bm{\omega}}}{u^{0}}.

From (
65
) and
a^{\mu}u_{\mu}=0
we obtain

\not{\partial}_{{\bm{\omega}}}\cdot{\bm{n}}+\partial_{\bm{u}}\cdot{\bm{a}}-\frac{{\bm{a}}\cdot{\bm{u}}}{(u^{0})^{2}}=-\frac{u_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu}}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}.

(66)

Substituting (
66
) in (
65
) we find that the constraint (
65
) is equivalent to choosing
a^{\mu}
as

a^{\mu}=\frac{1}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}\left(\delta_{\alpha}^{\mu}+u^{\mu}u_{\alpha}\right)\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\alpha\nu},

(67)

i.e., as the projection of the vector field
\mathcal{M}_{f}^{-1}\nabla_{b}(T_{f})^{ab}
onto the plane orthogonal to
u
.
Using (
67
) in (
66
) gives the following equation on
{\bm{n}}
:

\not{\partial}_{{\bm{\omega}}}\cdot{\bm{n}}=-4\frac{u_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu}}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}.

(68)

As the right hand side of (
68
) is independent of
{\bm{\omega}}\in S^{2}
, we may write
{\bm{n}}
as

{\bm{n}}(x,{\bm{u}},{\bm{\omega}})=-4\frac{u_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu}}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}{\bm{\xi}}(x,{\bm{u}},{\bm{\omega}}),

(69)

where the vector
{\bm{\xi}}(x,{\bm{u}},{\bm{\omega}})
satisfies

{\bm{\xi}}\cdot{\bm{\omega}}=0,\quad\not{\partial}_{{\bm{\omega}}}\cdot{\bm{\xi}}=1.

(70)

There exist of course infinitely many vectors
{\bm{\xi}}
satisfying (
70
). Before presenting one example, we derive the evolution equation satisfied by the particles number current
N_{f}
, which is independent of the choice of
{\bm{\xi}}
. This equation on
N_{f}
follows by
applying Lemma
2
with
\psi_{*}(x,p,s)=1
, which gives

\nabla_{\nu}(N_{f})^{\nu}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times S^{2}}\left(\not{\partial}_{{\bm{\omega}}}\cdot{\bm{n}}+\partial_{\bm{u}}\cdot{\bm{a}}-\frac{{\bm{a}}\cdot{\bm{u}}}{(u^{0})^{2}}\right)\,f_{\sigma}\,\frac{d{\bm{u}}\,d{\bm{\omega}}}{u^{0}}

and so, by (
66
),

\nabla_{\mu}(N_{f})^{\mu}=-\frac{(N_{f})_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu}}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}.

(71)

In conclusion, we have derived the following Vlasov equation on the kinetic density
f_{\sigma}=f_{\sigma}(x,{\bm{u}},{\bm{\omega}})
of particles with mass
m
and spin magnitude
\sigma
:

u^{\alpha}e_{(\alpha)}^{\mu}\partial_{x^{\mu}}f_{\sigma}+(\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}u^{\beta}+a^{i})\partial_{u^{i}}f_{\sigma}+\left(\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}\omega^{k}+n^{i}\right)\not{\partial}_{\omega^{i}}f_{\sigma}=0,

(72a)

where

a^{i}=\frac{1}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}\left(\delta_{\mu}^{i}+u^{i}u_{\mu}\right)\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu},\quad n^{i}=-4\frac{u_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu}}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}\xi^{i}

(72b)

and
{\bm{\xi}}=(\xi^{1},\xi^{2},\xi^{3})
is an arbitrary vector that satisfies (
70
). We also obtained that the evolution equation for the particles number current is (
71
).

When only one species of particles is present, the term
\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu}
in (
71
)-(
72
) must be replaced with
(J_{f})^{\nu}
in order for the constraint (
51
) to be satisfied. In particular, the Vlasov model for one species of particles derived in this section does not preserve the total number of particles.

Example of solution to (
70
)

The constraint
{\bm{\xi}}\cdot{\bm{\omega}}=0
implies that
{\bm{\xi}}

is the projection on the plane orthogonal to
{\bm{\omega}}
of a (dimensionless) vector field
{\bm{c}}(x,{\bm{u}},{\bm{\omega}})
, that is,

{\bm{\xi}}={\bm{\omega}}\wedge({\bm{\omega}}\wedge{\bm{c}}).

(73a)

A simple choice for the vector
{\bm{c}}
is to take it parallel to the relativistic velocity
{\bm{v}}={\bm{u}}/u^{0}
. Specifically, we make the
ansatz

{\bm{c}}=\zeta(z){\bm{v}},\quad z={\bm{\omega}}\cdot{\bm{v}}.

(73b)

Replacing the
ansatz
(
73
) into (
70
) and computing the divergence on
S^{2}
we obtain the following equation on the function
\zeta
:

\zeta^{\prime}(z)(z^{2}-|{\bm{v}}|^{2})+2z\zeta(z)=1,

the solution of which is

\zeta(z)=\frac{C-z}{|{\bm{v}}|^{2}-z^{2}},

where
C
is an arbitrary function of
|{\bm{v}}|
. Choosing
C\equiv 0
we arrive to the following final form of the vector
{\bm{\xi}}
:

{\bm{\xi}}=-\frac{{\bm{\omega}}\cdot{\bm{v}}}{|{\bm{\omega}}\wedge{\bm{v}}|^{2}}{\bm{\omega}}\wedge({\bm{\omega}}\wedge{\bm{v}}),

and therefore to the following final form of the vector
{\bm{n}}
:

{\bm{n}}=4\frac{u_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{f})^{\mu\nu}}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}\frac{{\bm{\omega}}\cdot{\bm{v}}}{|{\bm{\omega}}\wedge{\bm{v}}|^{2}}{\bm{\omega}}\wedge({\bm{\omega}}\wedge{\bm{v}}).

(74)

Remark
.

For the choice (
74
) of the vector
{\bm{n}}
, the assumption in Lemma
2
that the vector field
{\bm{n}}f_{\sigma}
should be smooth is satisfied when
f_{\sigma}=O(|{\bm{n}}\wedge{\bm{u}}|^{2})
as
|{\bm{n}}\wedge{\bm{u}}|\to 0
.

6
The Vlasov system for two species of particles

The Vlasov model derived in the previous section violates the conservation law of particles number. In this section we shall restore this fundamental property by postulating the existence in spacetime of a second species of particles interacting with the particles of the first species. We assume that the particles of the new species also have mass
m
and spin magnitude
\sigma
.

Let
\overline{f}_{\sigma}(x,{\bm{u}},{\bm{\omega}})
be the kinetic density of the new particles; the Vlasov equation on
\overline{f}_{\sigma}
is

u^{\alpha}e_{(\alpha)}^{\mu}\partial_{x^{\mu}}\overline{f}_{\sigma}+(\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}u^{\beta}+\overline{a}^{i})\partial_{u^{i}}\overline{f}_{\sigma}+\left(\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}\omega^{k}+\overline{n}^{i}\right)\not{\partial}_{\omega^{i}}\overline{f}_{\sigma}=0,

(75a)

where

\overline{a}^{i}=\frac{1}{\mathcal{M}_{\overline{f}}}\left(\delta_{\mu}^{i}+u^{i}u_{\mu}\right)\nabla_{\nu}(T_{\overline{f}})^{\mu\nu},\quad\overline{n}^{i}=-4\frac{u_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{\overline{f}})^{\mu\nu}}{\mathcal{M}_{\overline{f}}}\xi^{i}.

(75b)

The evolution equation for the number current of the new particles is therefore

\nabla_{\mu}(N_{\overline{f}})^{\mu}=-\frac{(N_{\overline{f}})_{\mu}\nabla_{\nu}(T_{\overline{f}})^{\mu\nu}}{\mathcal{M}_{\overline{f}}}.

(76)

As pointed out at the end of Section
4.2
, when two particle species are present it cannot be assumed that the conservation law of energy-momentum holds in the form (
51
) for each single species, since otherwise the constraint (
52
) would be violated.
To overcome this inconsistency, we postulate that the stress-energy tensors of the two particle species satisfy

\nabla_{b}(T_{f})^{ab}=\chi(x)(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{a},\quad\nabla_{b}(T_{\overline{f}})^{ab}=\overline{\chi}(x)(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{a},

(77)

for some functions
\chi,\overline{\chi}
such that

\chi(x)+\overline{\chi}(x)=1.

(78)

Replacing (
77
) in (
71
) and (
76
) we find the following equation on the total particles number current of the two species:

\nabla_{\mu}[(N_{f})^{\mu}+(N_{\overline{f}})^{\mu}]=-\chi(x)\frac{(N_{f})_{\mu}(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{\mu}}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}-\overline{\chi}(x)\frac{(N_{\overline{f}})_{\mu}(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{\mu}}{\mathcal{M}_{\overline{f}}}.

Hence, we obtain that
(N_{f})^{\mu}+(N_{\overline{f}})^{\mu}
is divergence-free when

\chi(x)\frac{(N_{f})_{\mu}(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{\mu}}{\mathcal{M}_{f}}+\overline{\chi}(x)\frac{(N_{\overline{f}})_{\mu}(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{\mu}}{\mathcal{M}_{\overline{f}}}=0.

(79)

Solving the system (
78
)-(
79
) and replacing the solution
\chi,\overline{\chi}
in (
77
) we obtain

\displaystyle\nabla_{b}(T_{f})^{ab}=-\frac{(N_{\overline{f}})_{\mu}(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{\mu}\mathcal{M}_{f}}{(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{\mu}(\mathcal{M}_{\overline{f}}(N_{f})_{\mu}-\mathcal{M}_{f}(N_{\overline{f}})_{\mu})}(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{a},

(80a)

\displaystyle\nabla_{b}(T_{\overline{f}})^{ab}=\frac{(N_{f})_{\mu}(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{\mu}\mathcal{M}_{\overline{f}}}{(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{\mu}(\mathcal{M}_{\overline{f}}(N_{f})_{\mu}-\mathcal{M}_{f}(N_{\overline{f}})_{\mu})}(J_{f+\overline{f}})^{a}.

(80b)

Thus, our final system on the kinetic densities
f_{\sigma},\overline{f}_{\sigma}
for the particles of the two species is (
72
)-(
75
) with (
80
) replaced in the definitions of the vectors
{\bm{a}},\overline{\bm{a}},{\bm{n}},\overline{\bm{n}}
. This system couples the dynamics of the kinetic densities
f_{\sigma},\overline{f}_{\sigma}
and is consistent with the conservation law of energy-momentum (
52
). The total number of particles of each individual species is not conserved, but their sum is.

7
Conclusions

In this paper we have introduced a new general relativistic kinetic model for the dynamics of spin neutral particles with positive mass in a spacetime with torsion. The main assumptions of the model are

(i)

the four-velocity
u
and four-spin
s
of the particles are constrained by
u^{\mu}s_{\mu}=0
and
s^{\mu}s_{\mu}=\sigma^{2}
for a positive constant
\sigma\neq 1
;

(ii)

the particles do not collide;

(iii)

the particles spin induces a torsion in spacetime that obeys Eintein-Cartan’s theory.

We have derived the most general transport equation on the kinetic particle density
f(x,u,s)
that is consistent with assumptions (i)-(ii); see (
63
). This equation is defined up to the choice of two arbitrary vectors, which can be chosen so that the model is compatible with the Bianchi identity (i.e., the conservation law of energy-momentum) in Einstein-Cartan’s theory. The total number of particles is not conserved by the single species particle model, which led us to assume the existence in spacetime of an additional species of particles with the same mass
m
and spin magnitude
\sigma
. The evolution equation for the kinetic density
\overline{f}(x,u,s)
of these new particles has been obtained by imposing that the total particles number current computed with the kinetic density
f+\overline{f}
should be divergence-free.

According to Equations (
72
)-(
75
), the particles motion is not geodesic. In fact, along their trajectory
x=x(\tau)
, the momentum
{\bm{p}}(\tau)=m{\bm{u}}(\tau)
of the two particle species satisfies, respectively,

\frac{dp^{i}}{d\tau}=m(\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}u^{\beta}+a^{i}),\quad\frac{dp^{i}}{d\tau}=m(\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.53441pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.77156pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.97969pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{\beta}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}u^{\beta}+\overline{a}^{i}),

where
a^{i},\overline{a}^{i}
are given by (
72b
), (
75b
). Thus, the deviation from geodesics motion is different for the particles of the two species: in one case it is determined by the force
m{\bm{a}}
, in the second case by the force
m\overline{{\bm{a}}}
. Both these forces are induced by the spacetime torsion.
Similarly, the direction
{\bm{\omega}}
of the rest frame spin vector for the two particle species obeys, respectively,

\frac{d\omega^{i}}{d\tau}=\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}\omega^{k}+n^{i},\quad\frac{d\omega^{i}}{d\tau}=\mathchoice{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}{\gamma^{{\mathchoice{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[5.19876pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[3.13454pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[2.23895pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}\mathchoice{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[4.42017pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[2.7052pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.93228pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}{i}}}_{{{\alpha}{k}\mathchoice{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\displaystyle$}}{\makebox[2.82928pt][c]{$\textstyle$}}{\makebox[1.68811pt][c]{$\scriptstyle$}}{\makebox[1.2058pt][c]{$\scriptscriptstyle$}}}}}u^{\alpha}\omega^{k}+\overline{n}^{i},

where
n^{i},\overline{n}^{i}
are given in (
72b
), (
75b
).
The first term in the right hand side of the previous equations is the change of
{\bm{\omega}}
due to the rotation of the frame
e_{(\mu)}
, while the terms
n^{i},\overline{n}^{i}
represent the rotation of
{\bm{\omega}}
induced by the spacetime torsion. Again, this effect is different for the two particle species.

In the typical applications of the Vlasov equation (
11
) for spinless particles,
f_{*}
stands for the kinetic density of galaxies, or even clusters of galaxies
[
2
]
. In our model, the kinetic densities
f_{\sigma}
,
\overline{f}_{\sigma}
are to be interpreted as the densities in state space of elementary particles. In this respect, the problem to which our model could be applicable is the study of the early universe dynamics.

Since we assume that particles are neutral and have positive mass, then our model applies, for instance, to neutrinos. Within this application it is tempting to identify the two particle species with neutrinos/antineutrinos pairs, but this interpretation is not without issues. In fact, according to the standard model neutrinos and antineutrinos are distinguished based on their weak isospin, and thus on their weak interaction, while in our model they differ by how torsion acts on them.
Moreover, the difference between the action of gravity on neutrinos and antineutrinos predicted by our model has never been observed.

Finally, we remark that the results presented in this paper are entirely different from those in
[
9
]
, which pertain to exact solutions of the standard Vlasov equation for particles without spin on a background symmetric manifold with a given simple torsion.

Acknowledgments.
I am grateful to Håkan Andréasson for his comments on this article.

Appendix A
Appendix

Proof of Lemma
1

We have

\displaystyle\partial_{u^{i}}\psi_{*}=\frac{u_{i}}{u^{0}}\,(\partial_{u^{0}}\psi)_{*}+(\partial_{u^{i}}\psi)_{*}+\sigma\omega_{i}(\partial_{s^{0}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{\sigma\omega_{i}u^{j}}{1+u^{0}}(\partial_{s^{j}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{\sigma{\bm{\omega}}\cdot{\bm{u}}}{1+u^{0}}(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{i}^{j}(\partial_{s^{j}}\psi)_{*},

(81)

\displaystyle\partial_{\omega^{i}}\psi_{*}=\sigma u_{i}(\partial_{s^{0}}\psi)_{*}+\sigma[(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})^{-1}]_{i}^{j}(\partial_{s^{j}}\psi)_{*}.

(82)

Inverting (
82
) we obtain

(\partial_{s^{j}}\psi)_{*}=-\frac{u_{j}}{u^{0}}(\partial_{s^{0}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{1}{\sigma}(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})^{k}_{j}\partial_{\omega^{k}}\psi_{*},

(83)

where we used that
(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})^{i}_{j}u_{i}=u_{j}/u^{0}
.
By (
83
) we have

u^{j}(\partial_{s^{j}}\psi)_{*}=-\frac{|{\bm{u}}|^{2}}{u^{0}}(\partial_{s^{0}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{u^{k}}{\sigma u^{0}}\partial_{\omega^{k}}\psi_{*}.

(84)

Using (
83
) and (
84
) in (
81
) we find, after some simplifications,

\displaystyle\partial_{u^{i}}\psi_{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{u_{i}}{u^{0}}(\partial_{u^{0}}\psi)_{*}+(\partial_{u^{i}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{s^{0}}{1+u^{0}}(\partial_{s^{i}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{s_{i}}{u^{0}}(\partial_{s^{0}}\psi)_{*}-\frac{s^{0}u_{i}}{(u^{0})^{2}(1+u^{0})}(\partial_{s^{0}}\psi)_{*}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})^{k}_{i}\omega_{k}}{u^{0}(1+u^{0})}u^{j}\partial_{\omega^{j}}\psi_{*}.

(85)

Hence, using
A^{\mu}u_{\mu}=A^{i}u_{i}-A^{0}u^{0}=0
, we obtain

\displaystyle A^{i}\partial_{u^{i}}\psi_{*}

\displaystyle=A^{\mu}(\partial_{u^{\mu}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{A^{\mu}s_{\mu}}{u^{0}}(\partial_{s^{0}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{s^{0}}{1+u^{0}}A^{\mu}(\partial_{s^{\mu}}\psi)_{*}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{ik}A^{i}\omega^{k}}{u^{0}(1+u^{0})}u^{j}\partial_{\omega^{j}}\psi_{*}.

(86)

Furthermore, again by (
83
),

\displaystyle A^{\mu}(\partial_{s^{\mu}}\psi)_{*}=\frac{1}{\sigma}(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{i}^{j}A^{i}\partial_{\omega^{j}}\psi_{*},

(87a)

\displaystyle B^{\mu}(\partial_{s^{\mu}}\psi)_{*}=\frac{A^{\mu}s_{\mu}}{u^{0}}(\partial_{s^{0}}\psi)_{*}+\frac{1}{\sigma}(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{i}^{j}B^{i}\partial_{\omega^{j}}\psi_{*},

(87b)

where for the second identity we used
A^{\mu}s_{\mu}+B^{\mu}u_{\mu}=0
.
Combining (
86
) and (
87
) we find

\displaystyle A^{\mu}(\partial_{u^{\mu}}\psi)_{*}+B^{\mu}(\partial_{s^{\mu}}\psi)_{*}=

\displaystyle A^{i}\partial_{u^{i}}\psi_{*}+\frac{(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{i}^{j}}{\sigma}\left(B^{i}-\frac{s^{0}}{1+u^{0}}A^{i}\right)\partial_{\omega^{j}}\psi_{*}

\displaystyle-\frac{(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{ik}A^{i}\omega^{k}}{u^{0}(1+u^{0})}u^{j}\partial_{\omega^{j}}\psi_{*}.

(88)

Now, in the right hand side of (
88
) we replace
\partial_{\omega^{j}}=\not{\partial}_{\omega^{j}}+\omega_{j}\omega^{k}\partial_{\omega^{k}}
to get

\displaystyle RHS~\eqref{temporalicchio}=

\displaystyle A^{i}\partial_{u^{i}}\psi_{*}+\frac{(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{i}^{j}}{\sigma}\left(B^{i}-\frac{s^{0}}{1+u^{0}}A^{i}\right)\not{\partial}_{\omega^{j}}\psi_{*}-\frac{(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{ik}A^{i}\omega^{k}}{u^{0}(1+u^{0})}u^{j}\not{\partial}_{\omega^{j}}\psi_{*}

\displaystyle+\frac{(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{i}^{j}}{u^{0}\sigma}\left(u^{0}B^{i}-s^{0}A^{i}\right)\omega_{j}\omega^{k}\partial_{\omega^{k}}\psi_{*}.

Since

(\sqrt{\mathfrak{h}})_{i}^{j}(u^{0}B^{i}-s^{0}A^{i})\omega_{j}=-\frac{s^{0}}{\sigma}\,(A^{\mu}v_{\mu}+B^{\mu}u_{\mu})=0,

the proof is complete.

Proof of Lemma
2

We have

e_{(\nu)}^{\beta}\partial_{x^{\beta}}\Psi^{\nu}=\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times S^{2}}f_{\sigma}\,u^{\nu}e_{(\nu)}^{\beta}\partial_{x^{\beta}}\psi_{*}\frac{d{\bm{u}}\,d{\bm{\omega}}}{u^{0}}+\int_{\mathbb{R}^{3}\times S^{2}}\psi_{*}u^{\nu}e_{(\nu)}^{\beta}\partial_{x^{\beta}}f_{\sigma}\,\frac{d{\bm{u}}\,d{\bm{\omega}}}{u^{0}}=I+II.

By (
63
) the second integral is

\displaystyle II

(We also make the tacit assumption that
f_{\sigma}
decays to zero as
|{\bm{u}}|\to\infty
sufficiently fast so that the boundary terms arising from the integration by parts in the
{\bm{u}}
variable vanish.)
The proof now follows from (
59
) with
y^{i}=n^{i}
.
